## Returned fault and its repair

The return against head 361332a8d0 is correct (C3/C4/E5). The old steps 5–8 identified the specified compact-support comparison with an ordered proper/open composite. The cited `lem-pur-compact-support-condensed-realization` states natural isomorphisms, coefficient coherences and refinement compatibility, but does not state the ordered factor equalities used. Its proof supplies a construction, which was improperly being treated as a further statement. In particular the old report's assertion that no conclusion was imported from that proof was wrong.

The new node isolates precisely that missing normalization as a mathematical obligation. It introduces the schemes, coefficients, ring sheaves, system adjunction, inputs and intermediate-domain bounds. Its system proper coefficient map followed by open scalar comparison defines b; q = L(b)u identifies the separately derived integral proper/open composite with the strict-tower functor. It states that q inverse gives the normalized integral coordinates. It then states both exact formulas: a = proper J comparison followed by open J comparison, preceded by J(q inverse); xi = proper coefficient comparison followed by open scalar comparison. The proper coefficient comparison is explicitly the adjunct of scalar extension of the proper counit, including F = Omega. Identity-open compactifications have identity open factors. Thus the item no longer extracts these facts from a different node's proof.

This is a smaller input, not the item restated: the new node has no endomorphism h, no simultaneous proper-unit arrow d, no equality between the resulting compact-support operators and no point realization b. The item's first four steps still construct the whole-system arrow and identify its limit; steps 5–7 use the normalization formulas to compare the unit and the explicit proper/open mate word; steps 8–10 compose those squares and handle the finite-matrix point comparison. No operator-coherence conclusion is assumed in the new node.

A concrete check explains why this repair is necessary. Take X to be two disjoint geometric points, f its proper structure map, h the map sending both points to the first, and the constant E sheaf M. Then compact cohomology is E squared and the proper-unit operator has matrix D = [[1,0],[1,0]]. A natural automorphism of the compact-support functor can multiply the second point summand by 2: Z = diag(1,2). This is natural in sheaves and compatible with compactification refinements, but ZD = [[1,0],[2,0]] differs from DZ = [[1,0],[1,0]]. Naturality and refinement coherence therefore do not fix the comparison needed by the item. The new proper/open factor formulas exclude this twist, already when f is proper and j is identity. This is a counterexample to the old inference, not to the unchanged item with its ordered comparison.

lem-zl-compact-support-realization-factor-normalization vs lem-sf-open-tensor-normalization: that statement supplies the open comparison and its adjunct normalization, but neither a proper factor nor identification with the specified adic compact-support comparison.

lem-zl-compact-support-realization-factor-normalization vs lem-sf-integral-compactification-comparison-bridge: that statement compares integral semilinear and constant-tower compact-support composition maps; it does not identify a or xi with their condensed proper/open factor formulas.

lem-zl-compact-support-realization-factor-normalization vs lem-pur-finite-free-compact-composition: that statement concerns etale finite quotient rings and composition compatibility of explicitly defined coefficient maps; it does not give an adic-to-condensed comparison or the E-to-Omega proper factor.

lem-zl-compact-support-realization-factor-normalization vs lem-a1-adic-compactification-section-realization: that statement concerns degree-zero compact-support sections for heart objects and open immersions, not the whole derived module-object proper/open comparison.

lem-zl-compact-support-realization-factor-normalization vs lem-zl-model-compact-comparison-unit: that statement identifies a specified model-Frobenius comparison with a condensed proper-unit operator, with no adic realization or coefficient normalization.

## Review by Q32

I read the item's statement, all ten delivered proof steps, the return entry, the compact-support comparison's entire statement and the proof construction that motivated the erroneous inference. The full separate server review.md is not present in the supplied sandbox or shared local review listing; the return's stated mathematical defect is reproduced and repaired above. For the source I read the local PDF at Definition 6.7.6, Lemma 6.7.7, Remark 6.7.8, Lemma 6.7.14, Proposition 6.8.14(2), and Remark 6.8.15. The compact-support construction derives proper image separately after open extension. The paper's rationalization equivalence alone does not identify the new module-object natural transformations. The new node's source place explicitly records this limitation, with no claim of a literal theorem or unproved source implication. For its intended proof, one would construct b on ringed diagram sites, take L(b)u, use completion to invert q, and identify the specified a and xi by their defining counit and open-unit maps. Finite free coefficient extension handles finite F; continuity on bounded-below inputs is needed for Omega. These are proof tasks for the new node, not facts used without a citation by this split. The cited Stacks open-tensor context is Tags 0GL1 and 0E8I (the respective lemma-j-shriek-and-tensor labels); neither states the whole new lemma.

Step 1 uses the locally-Noetherian finite-type, qcqs-over-affine and dimension statements, plus the definition of proper. They apply to finite-type schemes over k and to their proper compactifications. Nonnegative dimension bounds cover the empty case. The field characteristic ensures ell invertible.

Step 2 uses integral operations, the stratified-perfect characterization, integral Dbc = Dctf in the stability theorem, integral compact-support restriction and integral direct-image constructibility. Their statements supply membership and uniform bounds, rather than replacing an individual finite-level finite-Tor assumption by an unstated uniform hypothesis. Both integral notations and compatible etale-origin witnesses agree. Applying compact support to an open with identity proper part gives the open intermediate.

Step 3 uses simultaneous semilinear derived sections, semilinear proper/open mate pasting and ambient proper/open pasting to build the actual system unit and composition. The strict compact-support tower statement identifies the restricted system functor, including quotient transitions. Completed-operation tower substitution supplies the whole-system pullback reduction identification. I checked those are statements about transformations on systems; equality is not inferred from equal evaluations.

Step 4 uses integral compactification system realization and the comparison bridge for the exact integral unit/composition normalization. The strict-tower homotopy-limit statement gives a functor on derived tower morphisms. Thus D equals the integral word before any rational or point comparison. The new normalization is not used to replace this part of the argument.

Step 5 now cites the new q-coordinate and a-factor equations. The rational transport statement supplies extensions of functors and transformations, and the condensed E realization explicitly preserves units, counits and left compositors. Mate pasting supplies the right compositors. The open tensor statement gives the inverse open factor's actual adjunct normalization. No ordered factor equation is taken from the old compact-support comparison statement.

Step 6 now cites the new xi equation, its identity-open clause and its explicit counit-transpose formula. Therefore the proper comparison for h is the stated pi, not an arbitrary isomorphism supplied by naturality. I checked the direction of balancing: l goes from T Lh_E to Lh_Omega T and the adjunct defining pi uses its inverse. The unit identity follows by counit naturality and the triangle identity, as written in the step; its two adjuncts are the same identity. Exact coefficient extension and its adjunction are in the flat-coefficient calculus statement. The new lemma explicitly includes Omega, so finite freeness is not silently assumed at Omega.

Step 7 uses Nagata and common dense refinement with qcqs targets verified in step 1. The interchange uniqueness statement requires density and is applied only after choosing a dense compactification. The semilinear proper/open statement supplies the entire mate word and clopen identity/zero projection. Steps 5–6 compare its unit and counit factors, while the open normalization fixes the open factor. Pasting therefore compares this specific word. The old compact-support comparison is used only for its actually stated refinement compatibility when returning to the original choices. The new lemma, not that refinement assertion, fixes the ordered proper/open factors.

Step 8 explicitly composes the generator squares, and inverts A and c in the required directions. The actual module-object lower-shriek definition identifies its chosen-compactification functor with the separately derived proper/open composite. Step 9 uses exact geometric-point evaluation and the matrix functor statement: both send each finite matrix to that same matrix through E to Omega. The two finite-dimensional cohomology inputs are assumptions of the item. Step 10 composes the established naturality squares, and is elementary. Existing off-unit tools remain declared in split_report.tool_uses; the new lemma inherits this item's unit. Neither the fixed chapter nor the rationality unit's trace-to-determinant route is replaced.

I tested the new statement at the identity coefficient extension, identity open, identity proper morphism, zero input, empty open and the two-point proper example above. For identity extension the counit transpose is identity by the triangle equation; for identity open the open-unit factor is identity; empty open and zero input give zero complexes. Arbitrary scalar factors would fail the formulas and are not permitted. The boundedness and etale-witness hypotheses for the item's applications are exactly those checked in step 2. The coefficient ring at Omega is finite-subextension-valued throughout. No SGA route is used.

The new node carries no proof or proof dependencies; its searches are the five appended real search results, with the exact tool/query strings. I read the returned signatures and the local SheafOfModules.pullbackPushforwardAdjunction at PullbackContinuous.lean:60: it is an abelian sheaf adjunction, not the specified compact-support comparison. The other hits concern discrete condensed adjunctions or compactly supported functions. A second natural-language phrasing was run before concluding no match. All ten item steps are shorter than 2000 characters (the longest is step 7, 1581), with no long argument being labelled elementary to evade review. Every proof uses entry has a verbatim statement quotation. The server check_dag and review_split runs pass; a full dry run and direct delivery follow this final repair report.

For step 6 and the normalization statement, ChangeOfCoefficients.lean defines derivedPushHom as the extension/restriction adjunction mate of derivedComapLowerStarIso; derivedPullIso is its adjunction conjugate, and derivedOpenImmersionShriekIso is the conjugate along the open adjunction. derivedLowerShriekHom is, in order, the proper derivedPushHom and the open derivedOpenImmersionShriekIso, with the displayed associators. In ordinary function-composition notation this is exactly the ordered proper factor followed by Rg_* of the open factor in xi. Hence neither Omega nor its coefficient category is silently replaced by the full continuous-map ring or by the raw rationalization of an integral category.

```lean
module

public import Mathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.Limits
public import Mathlib.AlgebraicGeometry.Morphisms.WeaklyEtale
public import Mathlib.Topology.LocallyConstant.Algebra
public import WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.ModuleObjects.MapMod
public import WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.RingObjects.Condensed.ContinuousMap
public import WeilConjectures.FromJoel.CategoryTheory.Abelian.Flat.Basic
public import WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.FiniteCoefficient.UpperLowerStar.Coherence
public import WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.UpperLowerStar.Monoidal

/-!

# Condensed coefficient rings and their canonical coefficient morphisms

Let `Λ` be a condensed ring, i.e. a ring object of abelian sheaves on the pro-étale site of a
point `*_proét = (Profinite, coherent topology)`, for instance the condensed rings
`T̲ = C(-, T)` of `WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.RingObjects.Condensed.ContinuousMap`. Following
Sections 4 and 5 of `WeilConjectures/RefactorInstruction.tex`, this file pulls `Λ` back to every
scheme `X`,
```
Λ_X := p_X^* Λ,
```
along the canonical morphism `p_X : X_proét ⟶ *_proét`, and constructs explicitly the
isomorphisms of ring objects
```
α_f : f^* Λ_Y ≅ Λ_X
```
for every morphism of schemes `f : X ⟶ Y`. These are the coefficient rings and the canonical
coefficient morphisms of the six functors on module objects over `Λ_X`, built in the other files
of `WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject`.

## The profinite test objects

For a profinite set `S`, the ring `LC(S, ℤ)` of locally constant functions `S → ℤ` is the
filtered colimit of the finite products `ℤ^{S_i}` over the finite quotients `S_i` of `S`, so
```
Spec LC(S, ℤ) = lim_i ∐_{S_i} Spec ℤ,
```
the profinite set `S` regarded as a pro-étale scheme over `Spec ℤ`
(`AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.profiniteToProEt`). For a scheme `X` with structure morphism
`π_X : X ⟶ Spec ℤ`, the base change
```
X[S] := Spec LC(S, ℤ) ×_{Spec ℤ} X = lim_i ∐_{S_i} X
```
is the pro-étale `X`-scheme of the notes (`AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.profiniteToProEtOf`). The
functor `S ↦ X[S]` is the composite of `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` with the base change along `π_X`.

## The morphism `p_X` and the comparison `α_f`

Accordingly, the inverse image along `p_X` is the composite
```
p_X^* := π_X^* ∘ p^*,
```
where `p^* : Sheaf *_proét Ab ⥤ Sheaf (Spec ℤ)_proét Ab` is the inverse image along the
continuous functor `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` (`AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.pointUpperStar`). It is
strong monoidal and braided, so for a commutative condensed ring `Λ` the pullback
`Λ_X = p_X^* Λ` is a commutative ring object of abelian sheaves on `X_proét`
(`AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedRingObj`). For `f : X ⟶ Y`, the structure morphisms
satisfy `f ≫ π_Y = π_X`, `Spec ℤ` being terminal, and the comparison
```
f^* p_Y^* = f^* π_Y^* p^* ≅ (f ≫ π_Y)^* p^* = π_X^* p^* = p_X^*
```
is the composition isomorphism `ProEtale.upperStarComp` of the pullbacks, which on the test
objects is the canonical isomorphism `X ×_Y Y[S] ≅ X[S]` of the notes
(`AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.upperStarFromPointComp`).

## The canonical coefficient morphisms

* `α_f : f^* Λ_Y ⟶ Λ_X` (`AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedPullbackHom`): the component at
  `Λ` of the comparison `f^* ∘ p_Y^* ≅ p_X^*`. It is a morphism of ring objects, and an
  isomorphism for *every* `f`: the coefficients pulled back from the point form a
  Cartesian system. As a morphism of bundled ring objects it is
  `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.coefficientPullbackIso`.
* `η_f : Λ_Y ⟶ f_* Λ_X` (`AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedPushforwardHom`): the mate of
  `α_f` under the adjunction `f^* ⊣ f_*`, i.e. the unit `Λ_Y ⟶ f_* f^* Λ_Y` followed by `f_* α_f`.

This file also records the instance `AddCommGrpCat.hasFunctorialFlatResolution`, which the
derived functors on module objects need.

## Main definitions

* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.profiniteToProEt`: the functor `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` from
  profinite sets to the pro-étale site of `Spec ℤ`.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.pointUpperStar`: the inverse image `p^*` from condensed abelian
  groups to abelian sheaves on `(Spec ℤ)_proét`.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.upperStarFromPoint`: the inverse image `p_X^* = π_X^* ∘ p^*`.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedRingObj`: the ring object `Λ_X = p_X^* Λ`, and
  `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.coefficientRing`: the same as a bundled ring object.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.upperStarFromPointComp`: the natural isomorphism
  `f^* ∘ p_Y^* ≅ p_X^*`.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedPullbackHom` and
  `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedPushforwardHom`: the coefficient morphisms `α_f` and `η_f`.

## Main results

* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedPushforwardHom_id` and
  `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.condensedPushforwardHom_comp`: `η` is compatible with the identity
  and composition isomorphisms of the pushforwards. These are the cases `F = p^* Λ` of the general
  coherences `ProEtale.unitCompUpperStarComp_id` and `ProEtale.unitCompUpperStarComp_comp` of
  `WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.FiniteCoefficient.UpperLowerStar.Coherence`.

## Sorried input

* `AddCommGrpCat.hasFunctorialFlatResolution`: abelian groups admit functorial flat resolutions,
  by free abelian groups; it is what makes the derived functors with values in `Ab` available.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.weaklyEtale_toSpecZ_profiniteToScheme`: `Spec LC(S, ℤ) ⟶ Spec ℤ`
  is weakly étale, `LC(S, ℤ)` being ind-Zariski over `ℤ` (the filtered colimit of the finite
  products `ℤ^{S_i}`), and ind-Zariski algebras being weakly étale.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.preservesFiniteLimits_profiniteToProEt` and
  `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.coverPreserving_profiniteToProEt`: the functor
  `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` preserves finite limits (`LC(-, ℤ)` sends a fibre product of profinite
  sets to the tensor product, and the point to `ℤ`), and sends a finite jointly surjective
  family to a pro-étale covering (the map on underlying spaces of `Spec LC(S, ℤ)` is the
  projection `S × Spec ℤ → S`, so surjectivity is preserved, and the morphisms are weakly
  étale and affine). This is Lemma 4.2 of the notes.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.hasLeftKanExtension_op_profiniteToProEt`: the left Kan
  extensions along the opposite of `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` exist, both sites being essentially
  small in `u + 1` and `Ab.{u + 1}` being cocomplete for `u + 1`-small diagrams, exactly as
  for `SmallSite.hasLeftKanExtension_op_overPullback`.
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.isIso_η_pointUpperStar` and
  `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.isIso_δ_pointUpperStar`: `p^*` is strong monoidal, being the
  sheafification of a left Kan extension along a finite limit preserving functor, which
  commutes with the objectwise tensor product (as for `ProEtale.isIso_δ_upperStar`).
* `AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.isMonoidal_upperStarFromPointComp`: the comparison
  `f^* ∘ p_Y^* ≅ p_X^*` is a monoidal natural isomorphism. It is the mate of the composition
  isomorphism of pushforwards, which is monoidal by `ProEtale.isMonoidal_lowerStarComp`.

-/

@[expose] public section

universe u

open CategoryTheory Limits MonoidalCategory Opposite Functor.LaxMonoidal Functor.OplaxMonoidal

/-- Abelian groups admit functorial flat resolutions, by free abelian groups: the free abelian
group on the underlying set of `M` is flat, and surjects functorially onto `M`. -/
instance AddCommGrpCat.hasFunctorialFlatResolution :
    Abelian.HasFunctorialFlatResolution Ab.{u} :=
  sorry

namespace AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale

open Scheme

/-! ### `Spec ℤ` and the structure morphisms -/

/-- The terminal scheme `Spec ℤ`, in the universe `u`. -/
noncomputable abbrev specZ : Scheme.{u} :=
  Spec (.of (ULift.{u} ℤ))

/-- The structure morphism `π_X : X ⟶ Spec ℤ`. -/
noncomputable abbrev toSpecZ (X : Scheme.{u}) : X ⟶ specZ :=
  specULiftZIsTerminal.from X

/-- The structure morphisms are compatible with every morphism of schemes, `Spec ℤ` being
terminal. -/
lemma comp_toSpecZ {X Y : Scheme.{u}} (f : X ⟶ Y) : f ≫ toSpecZ Y = toSpecZ X :=
  specULiftZIsTerminal.hom_ext _ _

/-! ### Profinite sets as pro-étale schemes over `Spec ℤ` -/

/-- The functor `S ↦ LC(S, ℤ)` sending a profinite set to its ring of locally constant
integer-valued functions, contravariantly by precomposition. -/
noncomputable def profiniteToCommRingCatOp : Profinite.{u} ⥤ CommRingCat.{u}ᵒᵖ where
  obj S := op (CommRingCat.of (LocallyConstant S ℤ))
  map f := (CommRingCat.ofHom (LocallyConstant.comapRingHom f.hom.hom)).op

/-- The functor `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ) = lim_i ∐_{S_i} Spec ℤ`, the profinite set `S` regarded as a
scheme. -/
noncomputable def profiniteToScheme : Profinite.{u} ⥤ Scheme.{u} :=
  profiniteToCommRingCatOp ⋙ Scheme.Spec

/-- `Spec LC(S, ℤ) ⟶ Spec ℤ` is weakly étale. See the module docstring. -/
instance weaklyEtale_toSpecZ_profiniteToScheme (S : Profinite.{u}) :
    WeaklyEtale (toSpecZ (profiniteToScheme.obj S)) :=
  sorry

/-- The functor `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` from profinite sets to the pro-étale site of `Spec ℤ`. -/
noncomputable def profiniteToProEt : Profinite.{u} ⥤ specZ.{u}.ProEt where
  obj S := ProEt.mk (toSpecZ (profiniteToScheme.obj S))
  map f := MorphismProperty.Over.homMk (profiniteToScheme.map f) (comp_toSpecZ _)
  map_id S := MorphismProperty.Over.Hom.ext (profiniteToScheme.map_id S)
  map_comp f g := MorphismProperty.Over.Hom.ext (profiniteToScheme.map_comp f g)

lemma profiniteToProEt_obj_left (S : Profinite.{u}) :
    (profiniteToProEt.obj S).left = Spec (.of (LocallyConstant S ℤ)) :=
  rfl

/-- The functor `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` preserves finite limits. See the module docstring. -/
instance preservesFiniteLimits_profiniteToProEt : PreservesFiniteLimits profiniteToProEt.{u} :=
  sorry

instance : RepresentablyFlat profiniteToProEt.{u} :=
  flat_of_preservesFiniteLimits _

/-- The functor `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` sends finite jointly surjective families to pro-étale
coverings. See the module docstring. -/
lemma coverPreserving_profiniteToProEt :
    CoverPreserving (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) (ProEt.topology specZ) profiniteToProEt :=
  sorry

instance isContinuous_profiniteToProEt :
    profiniteToProEt.IsContinuous (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) (ProEt.topology specZ) :=
  Functor.isContinuous_of_coverPreserving (compatiblePreservingOfFlat _ _)
    coverPreserving_profiniteToProEt

/-- The left Kan extensions along the opposite of `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` exist. See the module
docstring. -/
instance hasLeftKanExtension_op_profiniteToProEt (F : Profinite.{u}ᵒᵖ ⥤ Ab.{u + 1}) :
    profiniteToProEt.op.HasLeftKanExtension F :=
  sorry

/-- The profinite test object `X[S] = Spec LC(S, ℤ) ×_{Spec ℤ} X = lim_i ∐_{S_i} X` over a
scheme `X`, as a functor from profinite sets to the pro-étale site of `X`. -/
noncomputable def profiniteToProEtOf (X : Scheme.{u}) : Profinite.{u} ⥤ X.ProEt :=
  profiniteToProEt ⋙
    MorphismProperty.Over.pullback @WeaklyEtale ⊤ (toSpecZ X)

/-! ### The inverse image `p^*` from the point -/

/-- The direct image `p_*` from abelian sheaves on `(Spec ℤ)_proét` to condensed abelian
groups, `(p_* G)(S) = G(Spec LC(S, ℤ))`. -/
noncomputable def pointLowerStar :
    Sheaf (ProEt.topology specZ.{u}) Ab.{u + 1} ⥤
      Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1} :=
  profiniteToProEt.sheafPushforwardContinuous Ab.{u + 1} (coherentTopology Profinite.{u})
    (ProEt.topology specZ)

/-- The inverse image `p^*` from condensed abelian groups to abelian sheaves on
`(Spec ℤ)_proét`: left Kan extension along `S ↦ Spec LC(S, ℤ)` followed by sheafification. -/
noncomputable def pointUpperStar :
    Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1} ⥤
      Sheaf (ProEt.topology specZ.{u}) Ab.{u + 1} :=
  Functor.sheafPullbackConstruction.sheafPullback profiniteToProEt Ab.{u + 1}
    (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) (ProEt.topology specZ)

/-- The adjunction `p^* ⊣ p_*`. -/
noncomputable def pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction : pointUpperStar.{u} ⊣ pointLowerStar :=
  Functor.sheafPullbackConstruction.sheafAdjunctionContinuous profiniteToProEt Ab.{u + 1}
    (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) (ProEt.topology specZ)

/-- The direct image `p_*` is lax monoidal, being the pushforward along a continuous functor. -/
noncomputable instance laxMonoidal_pointLowerStar : pointLowerStar.{u}.LaxMonoidal := by
  unfold pointLowerStar
  infer_instance

/-- The inverse image `p^*` is oplax monoidal, being left adjoint to the lax monoidal `p_*`. -/
noncomputable instance oplaxMonoidal_pointUpperStar : pointUpperStar.{u}.OplaxMonoidal :=
  pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.leftAdjointOplaxMonoidal

/-- The inverse image `p^*` preserves the unit. See the module docstring. -/
instance isIso_η_pointUpperStar : IsIso (η pointUpperStar.{u}) :=
  sorry

/-- The inverse image `p^*` commutes with the tensor product. See the module docstring. -/
instance isIso_δ_pointUpperStar (F G : Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1}) :
    IsIso (δ pointUpperStar F G) :=
  sorry

/-- The inverse image `p^*` is strong monoidal. -/
noncomputable instance monoidal_pointUpperStar : pointUpperStar.{u}.Monoidal :=
  Functor.Monoidal.ofOplaxMonoidal _

/-- The direct image `p_*` is lax braided, being the pushforward along a continuous functor. -/
noncomputable instance laxBraided_pointLowerStar : pointLowerStar.{u}.LaxBraided := by
  unfold pointLowerStar
  infer_instance

/-- The inverse image `p^*` is lax braided (hence braided, being strong monoidal): the
compatibility with the braiding is the mate of the one of `p_*`. In particular `p^*` sends
commutative ring objects to commutative ring objects. -/
noncomputable instance laxBraided_pointUpperStar : pointUpperStar.{u}.LaxBraided where
  braided F G := by
    have h : δ pointUpperStar F G ≫ (β_ (pointUpperStar.obj F) (pointUpperStar.obj G)).hom =
        pointUpperStar.map (β_ F G).hom ≫ δ pointUpperStar G F := by
      change (pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.homEquiv _ _).symm
          ((pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.unit.app F ⊗ₘ
            pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.unit.app G) ≫ μ pointLowerStar _ _) ≫ _ =
        _ ≫ (pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.homEquiv _ _).symm
          ((pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.unit.app G ⊗ₘ
            pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.unit.app F) ≫ μ pointLowerStar _ _)
      rw [← Adjunction.homEquiv_naturality_right_symm]
      erw [← Adjunction.homEquiv_naturality_left_symm]
      rw [Category.assoc, Functor.LaxBraided.braided]
      erw [BraidedCategory.braiding_naturality_assoc]
      rfl
    rw [← cancel_epi (δ pointUpperStar F G), Functor.Monoidal.δ_μ_assoc, reassoc_of% h,
      Functor.Monoidal.δ_μ, Category.comp_id]

/-! ### The inverse image `p_X^*` and the coefficient rings `Λ_X` -/

/-- The inverse image `p_X^* = π_X^* ∘ p^*` from condensed abelian groups to abelian sheaves on
`X_proét`. On the test objects it is the inverse image along `S ↦ X[S]`. -/
noncomputable def upperStarFromPoint (X : Scheme.{u}) :
    Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1} ⥤ Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1} :=
  pointUpperStar ⋙ (toSpecZ X)^*[Ab.{u + 1}]

/-- The inverse image `p_X^*` is strong monoidal. -/
noncomputable instance monoidal_upperStarFromPoint (X : Scheme.{u}) :
    (upperStarFromPoint X).Monoidal := by
  unfold upperStarFromPoint
  infer_instance

/-- The inverse image `p_X^*` is lax braided. In particular it sends commutative ring objects to
commutative ring objects. -/
noncomputable instance laxBraided_upperStarFromPoint (X : Scheme.{u}) :
    (upperStarFromPoint X).LaxBraided := by
  unfold upperStarFromPoint
  infer_instance

/-- The coefficient ring `Λ_X = p_X^* Λ` on `X_proét` of a condensed ring `Λ`. -/
noncomputable def coefficientRing (X : Scheme.{u})
    (Λ : Mon (Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1})) :
    Mon (Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) :=
  (upperStarFromPoint X).mapMon.obj Λ

lemma coefficientRing_X (X : Scheme.{u})
    (Λ : Mon (Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1})) :
    (coefficientRing X Λ).X = (upperStarFromPoint X).obj Λ.X :=
  rfl

/-- The condensed ring `T̲ = C(-, T)` of continuous maps into a topological ring `T`, as a ring
object of condensed abelian groups. -/
noncomputable def continuousMapCondensedRing (T : Type) [TopologicalSpace T] [Ring T]
    [IsTopologicalRing T] : Mon (Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1}) :=
  Mon.mk (Profinite.continuousMapRingObj T)

/-! ### The comparison `α_f : f^* Λ_Y ≅ Λ_X` -/

section Comparison

variable {X Y : Scheme.{u}} (f : X ⟶ Y)

/-- The natural isomorphism `f^* ∘ p_Y^* ≅ p_X^*`: the composition isomorphism
`f^* ∘ π_Y^* ≅ (f ≫ π_Y)^*` of the pullbacks, followed by `f ≫ π_Y = π_X`. On the test objects
it is the canonical isomorphism `X ×_Y Y[S] ≅ X[S]`. -/
noncomputable def upperStarFromPointComp :
    upperStarFromPoint Y ⋙ (f)^*[Ab.{u + 1}] ≅ upperStarFromPoint X :=
  Functor.associator _ _ _ ≪≫ Functor.isoWhiskerLeft pointUpperStar
    ((upperStarComp Ab.{u + 1} f (toSpecZ Y)).symm ≪≫
      eqToIso (congrArg (upperStar Ab.{u + 1}) (comp_toSpecZ f)))

/-- The comparison `f^* ∘ p_Y^* ≅ p_X^*` is a monoidal natural isomorphism. See the module
docstring. -/
instance isMonoidal_upperStarFromPointComp :
    NatTrans.IsMonoidal (upperStarFromPointComp f).hom :=
  sorry

/-- The comparison `α_f : f^* Λ_Y ≅ Λ_X` of the coefficient rings pulled back from the point,
an isomorphism of ring objects of abelian sheaves on `X_proét`. -/
noncomputable def coefficientPullbackIso
    (Λ : Mon (Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1})) :
    (f)^*[Ab.{u + 1}].mapMon.obj (coefficientRing Y Λ) ≅ coefficientRing X Λ :=
  (Functor.mapMonCompIso (F := upperStarFromPoint Y) (G := (f)^*[Ab.{u + 1}])).symm.app Λ ≪≫
    (Functor.mapMonNatIso (upperStarFromPointComp f)).app Λ

end Comparison

/-! ### The canonical coefficient morphisms of the six functors on module objects -/

section CoefficientMorphisms

open MonObj

open scoped CategoryTheory.Obj

variable (Λ : Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1}) [MonObj Λ]

/-! ### The coefficient ring objects `Λ_X` -/

/-- The coefficient ring object `Λ_X = p_X^* Λ` of abelian sheaves on `X_proét`, with the ring
object structure transported along the strong monoidal functor `p_X^*`. -/
noncomputable abbrev condensedRingObj (X : Scheme.{u}) :
    Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1} :=
  (upperStarFromPoint X).obj Λ

/-- The coefficient ring object `Λ_X` is commutative when `Λ` is, `p_X^*` being braided. -/
instance isCommMonObj_condensedRingObj [IsCommMonObj Λ] (X : Scheme.{u}) :
    IsCommMonObj (condensedRingObj Λ X) :=
  Functor.isCommMonObj_obj (F := upperStarFromPoint X)

variable {X Y : Scheme.{u}} (f : X ⟶ Y)

/-! ### The canonical coefficient morphisms -/

/-- The canonical coefficient morphism `α_f : f^* Λ_Y ⟶ Λ_X`: the component at `Λ` of the
comparison isomorphism `f^* ∘ p_Y^* ≅ p_X^*`, which on the test objects is the canonical
isomorphism `X ×_Y Y[S] ≅ X[S]`. -/
noncomputable abbrev condensedPullbackHom :
    (f)^*[Ab.{u + 1}].obj (condensedRingObj Λ Y) ⟶ condensedRingObj Λ X :=
  Functor.compAlgHom (upperStarFromPoint Y) (f)^*[Ab.{u + 1}] (upperStarFromPointComp f).hom Λ

/-- `α_f` is a morphism of ring objects. -/
instance isMonHom_condensedPullbackHom : IsMonHom (condensedPullbackHom Λ f) :=
  Functor.isMonHom_compAlgHom _ _ _ _

/-- `α_f` is an isomorphism, for every morphism of schemes `f`. -/
instance isIso_condensedPullbackHom : IsIso (condensedPullbackHom Λ f) :=
  inferInstanceAs (IsIso ((upperStarFromPointComp f).hom.app Λ))

omit [MonObj Λ] in
/-- For an open immersion `j : U ⟶ X`, `α_j : j^* Λ_X ⟶ Λ_U` is an isomorphism. This is the
special case of `ProEtale.isIso_condensedPullbackHom` used by the exceptional operations. -/
lemma isIso_condensedPullbackHom_of_isOpenImmersion {U X : Scheme.{u}} (j : U ⟶ X)
    [IsOpenImmersion j] : IsIso (condensedPullbackHom Λ j) :=
  inferInstance

/-- The canonical coefficient morphism `η_f : Λ_Y ⟶ f_* Λ_X`: the mate of `α_f` under the
adjunction `f^* ⊣ f_*`, the unit `Λ_Y ⟶ f_* f^* Λ_Y` followed by `f_* α_f`. -/
noncomputable def condensedPushforwardHom :
    condensedRingObj Λ Y ⟶ (f)_*[Ab.{u + 1}].obj (condensedRingObj Λ X) :=
  (ProEtale.upperStarLowerStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} f).unitAlgHom (condensedRingObj Λ Y) ≫
    (f)_*[Ab.{u + 1}].map (condensedPullbackHom Λ f)

/-- `η_f` is a morphism of ring objects, for the ring object structure of `f_* Λ_X` induced by
the lax monoidal structure of `f_*`. -/
instance isMonHom_condensedPushforwardHom : IsMonHom (condensedPushforwardHom Λ f) :=
  inferInstanceAs (IsMonHom
    ((ProEtale.upperStarLowerStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} f).unitAlgHom (condensedRingObj Λ Y) ≫
      (f)_*[Ab.{u + 1}].map (condensedPullbackHom Λ f)))

omit [MonObj Λ] in
/-- `η_f` is the mate of `α_f`: it is the unit `Λ_Y ⟶ f_* f^* Λ_Y` followed by `f_* α_f`. -/
@[reassoc]
lemma unitAlgHom_comp_map_condensedPullbackHom :
    (ProEtale.upperStarLowerStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} f).unitAlgHom (condensedRingObj Λ Y) ≫
      (f)_*[Ab.{u + 1}].map (condensedPullbackHom Λ f) =
        condensedPushforwardHom Λ f :=
  rfl

omit [MonObj Λ] in
/-- The canonical coefficient morphism `α_f` is the composition isomorphism
`f^* π_Y^* ≅ (f ≫ π_Y)^*` of the pullbacks, followed by `f ≫ π_Y = π_X`, at `p^* Λ`. -/
lemma condensedPullbackHom_eq :
    condensedPullbackHom Λ f =
      (upperStarComp Ab.{u + 1} f (toSpecZ Y)).inv.app (pointUpperStar.obj Λ) ≫
        (eqToHom (congrArg (upperStar Ab.{u + 1}) (comp_toSpecZ f))).app
          (pointUpperStar.obj Λ) := by
  have h₁ : condensedPullbackHom Λ f = (upperStarFromPointComp f).hom.app Λ := rfl
  have h₂ : upperStarFromPointComp f =
      Functor.associator pointUpperStar (toSpecZ Y)^*[Ab.{u + 1}] (f)^*[Ab.{u + 1}] ≪≫
        Functor.isoWhiskerLeft pointUpperStar ((upperStarComp Ab.{u + 1} f (toSpecZ Y)).symm ≪≫
          eqToIso (congrArg (upperStar Ab.{u + 1}) (comp_toSpecZ f))) := by
    unfold upperStarFromPointComp
    rfl
  rw [h₁, h₂]
  exact associator_isoWhiskerLeft_upperStarComp_hom_app Ab.{u + 1} pointUpperStar f _ _
    (comp_toSpecZ f) Λ

omit [MonObj Λ] in
/-- `η` of the identity is the identification `Λ_X ≅ (𝟙 X)_* Λ_X`. -/
lemma condensedPushforwardHom_id (X : Scheme.{u}) :
    condensedPushforwardHom Λ (𝟙 X) =
      (ProEtale.lowerStarId Ab.{u + 1} X).inv.app (condensedRingObj Λ X) := by
  rw [condensedPushforwardHom, condensedPullbackHom_eq]
  exact unitCompUpperStarComp_id Ab.{u + 1} (toSpecZ X) (comp_toSpecZ (𝟙 X))
    (pointUpperStar.obj Λ)

omit [MonObj Λ] in
/-- `η` of a composition is the composition of the `η`'s, up to the composition isomorphism
`g_* ∘ f_* ≅ (f ≫ g)_*`. -/
lemma condensedPushforwardHom_comp {Z : Scheme.{u}} (g : Y ⟶ Z) :
    condensedPushforwardHom Λ (f ≫ g) =
      condensedPushforwardHom Λ g ≫ (g)_*[Ab.{u + 1}].map (condensedPushforwardHom Λ f) ≫
        (ProEtale.lowerStarComp Ab.{u + 1} f g).hom.app (condensedRingObj Λ X) := by
  rw [condensedPushforwardHom, condensedPushforwardHom, condensedPushforwardHom,
    condensedPullbackHom_eq, condensedPullbackHom_eq, condensedPullbackHom_eq]
  exact unitCompUpperStarComp_comp Ab.{u + 1} f g (toSpecZ X) (toSpecZ Y) (toSpecZ Z)
    (comp_toSpecZ f) (comp_toSpecZ g) (comp_toSpecZ (f ≫ g)) (pointUpperStar.obj Λ)

end CoefficientMorphisms

end AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale
```

<summary>WeilConjectures/SixFunctors/ProEtale/ModuleObject/BaseChangeTheorems/MorphismDefs.lean</summary>

```lean
/-
Copyright (c) 2026 Nailin Guan. All rights reserved.
Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
Authors: Nailin Guan
-/
module

public import WeilConjectures.Mathlib.CategoryTheory.Functor.SquareTransport
public import WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.Derived.BoundedConstructible

/-!

# The base-change morphism on bounded constructible module objects over `Λ`

Let `Λ` be a commutative condensed ring. Fix a commutative square of schemes

```
X' --g'--> X
|          |
f'         f
v          v
Y' --g---> Y
```

recorded as `sq : CommSq g' f' f g`, of schemes separated and of finite type over a field `k` of
characteristic `p > 0` with `p ≠ ℓ` and of morphisms over `Spec k`, as for the bounded
constructible six functors. This file constructs the *base-change morphism*

`BC_sq : Rf_* ⋙ Lg^* ⟶ Lg'^* ⋙ Rf'_*` (in diagrammatic `⋙`-order)

on the bounded constructible derived categories `Dᵇc(-)⟦Λ⟧` of module objects over
`Λ_X = p_X^* Λ`
(`WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.Derived.BoundedConstructible`),
exactly as the étale base-change morphism `Etale.baseChange` of
`WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.Etale.BaseChangeTheorems.MorphismDefs`: it is the composite of
three named transformations, unit insertion, square transport along the pullback comparison
`θ_sq : Lf'^* ∘ Lg^* ≅ Lg'^* ∘ Lf^*`, and counit contraction, for the restricted adjunctions
`Lf^* ⊣ Rf_*` on `Dᵇc(-)⟦Λ⟧`.

The pullback comparison is built from the composition isomorphisms
`ProEtale.CondensedRing.boundedConstructibleUpperStarComp`, which come from the levelwise
functors `D((h)^*⟦Λ⟧)` since the pullback on module objects is exact.

## Main definitions

- `ProEtale.CondensedRing.boundedConstructiblePullbackSquareComparison`: the pullback
  comparison `θ_sq` of a commutative square on `Dᵇc(-)⟦Λ⟧`.
- `ProEtale.CondensedRing.boundedConstructibleBaseChangeUnitStep`,
  `ProEtale.CondensedRing.boundedConstructibleBaseChangeSquareStep`,
  `ProEtale.CondensedRing.boundedConstructibleBaseChangeCounitStep`: the three steps.
- `ProEtale.CondensedRing.boundedConstructibleBaseChange`: the base-change morphism `BC_sq`.

-/

@[expose] public section

universe u

open scoped CategoryTheory.Obj

open CategoryTheory Limits

open scoped CategoryTheory.Functor

namespace AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.CondensedRing

open Scheme

variable (Λ : Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1}) [MonObj Λ] [IsCommMonObj Λ]
  (ℓ : ℕ) [Fact ℓ.Prime] (k : Type u) [Field k] [Fact (ringChar k).Prime] [Fact (ringChar k ≠ ℓ)]

variable {X Y X' Y' : Scheme.{u}} [X.Over (Spec (.of k))] [Y.Over (Spec (.of k))]
  [X'.Over (Spec (.of k))] [Y'.Over (Spec (.of k))]
  [LocallyOfFiniteType (X ↘ Spec (.of k))] [QuasiCompact (X ↘ Spec (.of k))]
  [IsSeparated (X ↘ Spec (.of k))]
  [LocallyOfFiniteType (Y ↘ Spec (.of k))] [QuasiCompact (Y ↘ Spec (.of k))]
  [IsSeparated (Y ↘ Spec (.of k))]
  [LocallyOfFiniteType (X' ↘ Spec (.of k))] [QuasiCompact (X' ↘ Spec (.of k))]
  [IsSeparated (X' ↘ Spec (.of k))]
  [LocallyOfFiniteType (Y' ↘ Spec (.of k))] [QuasiCompact (Y' ↘ Spec (.of k))]
  [IsSeparated (Y' ↘ Spec (.of k))]

include ℓ k

section PullbackComparison

variable {f : X ⟶ Y} {g : Y' ⟶ Y} {f' : X' ⟶ Y'} {g' : X' ⟶ X} [f.IsOver (Spec (.of k))]
  [g.IsOver (Spec (.of k))] [f'.IsOver (Spec (.of k))] [g'.IsOver (Spec (.of k))]

/-- The pullback comparison `θ_sq : Lf'^* ∘ Lg^* ≅ Lg'^* ∘ Lf^*` of a commutative square
`g' ≫ f = f' ≫ g` of schemes on the bounded constructible derived categories, obtained from the
composition isomorphisms `boundedConstructibleUpperStarComp` and the commutativity of the
square. -/
noncomputable def boundedConstructiblePullbackSquareComparison (sq : CommSq g' f' f g) :
    boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k f' ≅
      boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g' :=
  (boundedConstructibleUpperStarComp Λ ℓ k f' g).symm ≪≫
    eqToIso (by simp only [sq.w]) ≪≫ boundedConstructibleUpperStarComp Λ ℓ k g' f

end PullbackComparison

section BaseChange

variable (f : X ⟶ Y) (g : Y' ⟶ Y) (f' : X' ⟶ Y') (g' : X' ⟶ X) [f.IsOver (Spec (.of k))]
  [g.IsOver (Spec (.of k))] [f'.IsOver (Spec (.of k))] [g'.IsOver (Spec (.of k))]

/-- The *unit insertion* step of the base-change morphism on bounded constructible complexes:
the natural transformation `Lg^* ∘ Rf_* ⟶ Rf'_* ∘ Lf'^* ∘ Lg^* ∘ Rf_*` obtained by inserting the
unit of the adjunction `Lf'^* ⊣ Rf'_*`. -/
noncomputable def boundedConstructibleBaseChangeUnitStep :
    boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g ⟶
      (boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g) ⋙
        (boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k f' ⋙ boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f') :=
  (Functor.rightUnitor _).inv ≫ Functor.whiskerLeft
    (boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g)
    (boundedConstructibleUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction Λ ℓ k f').unit

variable {f g f' g'} in
/-- The *square transport* step of the base-change morphism on bounded constructible
complexes: `Functor.squareTransport` applied to the pullback comparison `θ_sq`, under `Rf_*` on
the left and `Rf'_*` on the right. -/
noncomputable def boundedConstructibleBaseChangeSquareStep (sq : CommSq g' f' f g) :
    (boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g) ⋙
        (boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k f' ⋙ boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f') ≅
      (boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k f) ⋙
        (boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g' ⋙ boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f') :=
  Functor.squareTransport (boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f)
    (boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f') (boundedConstructiblePullbackSquareComparison Λ ℓ k sq)

/-- The *counit contraction* step of the base-change morphism on bounded constructible
complexes: the natural transformation obtained by contracting the counit of the adjunction
`Lf^* ⊣ Rf_*`. -/
noncomputable def boundedConstructibleBaseChangeCounitStep :
    (boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k f) ⋙
        (boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g' ⋙ boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f') ⟶
      boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g' ⋙ boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f' :=
  Functor.whiskerRight (boundedConstructibleUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction Λ ℓ k f).counit
    (boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g' ⋙ boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f') ≫
    (Functor.leftUnitor _).hom

variable {f g f' g'} in
/-- The *base-change morphism* `BC_sq : Lg^* ∘ Rf_* ⟶ Rf'_* ∘ Lg'^*` of a commutative square
`g' ≫ f = f' ≫ g` of schemes on the bounded constructible derived categories of module objects
over `Λ`, assembled as the composite of the unit insertion, the square transport, and the
counit contraction.

Proper base change asserts that this morphism is an isomorphism for a cartesian square with `f`
proper; no such hypothesis is built into the definition. -/
@[no_expose]
noncomputable def boundedConstructibleBaseChange (sq : CommSq g' f' f g) :
    boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f ⋙ boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g ⟶
      boundedConstructibleUpperStar Λ ℓ k g' ⋙ boundedConstructibleLowerStar Λ ℓ k f' :=
  boundedConstructibleBaseChangeUnitStep Λ ℓ k f g f' ≫
    (boundedConstructibleBaseChangeSquareStep Λ ℓ k sq).hom ≫
      boundedConstructibleBaseChangeCounitStep Λ ℓ k f f' g'

end BaseChange

end AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.CondensedRing
```

</details>

<summary>WeilConjectures/SixFunctors/ProEtale/ModuleObject/Colimit/Compatibility.lean</summary>

```lean
/-
Copyright (c) 2026 Nailin Guan. All rights reserved.
Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
Authors: Nailin Guan
-/
module

public import WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.RingObjects.Condensed.FiniteSubextension
public import WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.ChangeOfCoefficients

/-!

# Compatibility of the six functors with the extension of `ℓ`-adic coefficients

Let `ℓ` be a prime, `E ≤ F` finite subextensions of `ℚ_ℓ` inside `ℚ̄_ℓ`
(`PadicAlgCl.FiniteIntermediateField ℓ`) and `Ω = ℚ̄_ℓ`. The inclusions of condensed rings
`E̲ ⟶ F̲` (`Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjMap`) and `E̲ ⟶ ℚ̲̄_ℓ`
(`Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl`) of
`WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.RingObjects.Condensed.FiniteSubextension` pull back, on a
scheme `X`, to coefficient changes `ι : E_X ⟶ F_X` and `ι : E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X`
(`ProEtale.coefficientMap`), inducing the derived extensions of scalars
```
T^X_{E,F} = F_X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} - ,   T^X_{E,Ω} = ℚ̄_ℓ,X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} - .
```
The comparisons (Pull), (Push) and (Shriek) between these and the derived functors `Lf^*`,
`Rf_*`, `Rf_!` on module objects are the ones of
`WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.ChangeOfCoefficients`, for these two
coefficient changes. This file supplies the coefficient-specific input:

* `ℚ̄_ℓ,X = colim_E E_X` (`ProEtale.isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone`), the pullback along the
  left adjoint `p_X^*` of `ℚ̲̄_ℓ = colim_E E̲` (`Profinite.isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone`);
* both coefficient changes are flat, so that (Pull) and (Push) are defined on the unbounded
  derived categories, and (Pull) is an isomorphism there;
* for a finite extension `F/E`, (Push) is an isomorphism on all unbounded objects, hence so is
  (Shriek);
* for `E ⊆ Ω`, (Push) is an isomorphism on all unbounded objects when `f` is proper, but not
  for an arbitrary `f`. Since the proper part of a compactification is proper, (Shriek) is an
  isomorphism on all unbounded objects for every `f` with a compactification.

## Sorried input

* **(A0.2)** `ProEtale.CondensedRing.preservesFiniteLimits_extendScalars_padicSubfieldRingObjMap`:
  for finite subextensions `E ≤ F`, the extension of scalars `F_X ⊗_{E_X} -` is exact, i.e.
  `F_X` is flat over `E_X`. Indeed `F_X` is finite free over `E_X`: an `E`-basis `b₁, …, bₙ` of
  `F` gives `C(-, F) ≅ C(-, E)^n`, `h ↦ (hᵢ)` with `h = ∑ hᵢ bᵢ`, on the point, and `p_X^*`
  preserves finite direct sums.
* **(A0.2)**
  `ProEtale.CondensedRing.preservesFiniteLimits_extendScalars_padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl`:
  for a finite subextension `E`, the extension of scalars `ℚ̄_ℓ,X ⊗_{E_X} -` is exact, i.e.
  `ℚ̄_ℓ,X` is flat over `E_X`: it is the filtered colimit of the `F_X`, `F ≥ E`
  (`ProEtale.isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone`), each of which is finite free over `E_X`.
* `ProEtale.CondensedRing.isIso_derivedPushHom_padicSubfieldRingObjMap`: for a finite extension
  `F/E`, (Push) is an isomorphism on all unbounded objects, since `F_X ≅ E_X^n` reduces it to
  the compatibility of `Rf_*` with finite direct sums.
* `ProEtale.CondensedRing.isIso_derivedPushHom_padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl_of_isProper`:
  for `E ⊆ Ω` and `f` proper, (Push) is an isomorphism on all unbounded objects: `Rf_*` has a
  right adjoint `Rf^!`, hence commutes with the coproducts through which `ℚ̄_ℓ,X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} -` is
  computed from the finite stages. For a general `f` this fails, and (Push) is an isomorphism
  only at objects at which `Rf_*` commutes with the relevant coproducts; no such statement is
  made here.

-/

@[expose] public section

universe u

open CategoryTheory Limits

open scoped CategoryTheory.Obj CategoryTheory.Functor

namespace AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale

open Scheme PadicAlgCl

/-! ### The coefficient rings `E_X`, `ℚ̄_ℓ,X` and their inclusions -/

section RingObj

variable (X : Scheme.{u}) (ℓ : ℕ) [Fact ℓ.Prime]

/-- The coefficient ring object `E_X = p_X^* E̲` on `X_proét`, for a finite subextension
`E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ` of `ℚ_ℓ`. -/
noncomputable abbrev padicSubfieldRingObj (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) :
    Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1} :=
  condensedRingObj (Profinite.continuousMapRingObj E) X

/-- The coefficient ring object `ℚ̄_ℓ,X = p_X^* ℚ̲̄_ℓ` on `X_proét`. -/
noncomputable abbrev padicAlgClRingObj : Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1} :=
  condensedRingObj (Profinite.padicAlgClRingObj ℓ) X

/-- The inclusion `E_X ⟶ F_X` for finite subextensions `E ≤ F`, the coefficient change induced by
the inclusion `E̲ ⟶ F̲` of condensed rings. -/
noncomputable abbrev padicSubfieldRingObjMap {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E ⟶ padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ F :=
  coefficientMap (Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjMap ℓ h) X

/-- The inclusion `E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X` for a finite subextension `E`, the coefficient change induced by
the inclusion `E̲ ⟶ ℚ̲̄_ℓ` of condensed rings. -/
noncomputable abbrev padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) :
    padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E ⟶ padicAlgClRingObj X ℓ :=
  coefficientMap (Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl ℓ E) X

/-- The inclusion `E_X ⟶ F_X` followed by the inclusion `F_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X` is the inclusion
`E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X`. -/
@[reassoc]
lemma padicSubfieldRingObjMap_comp_toPadicAlgCl {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    padicSubfieldRingObjMap X ℓ h ≫ padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl X ℓ F =
      padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E := by
  rw [← coefficientMap_comp, Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjMap_comp_toPadicAlgCl]

/-- The inverse image `p_X^*` from condensed abelian groups is a left adjoint, being the
composite `π_X^* ∘ p^*` of two left adjoints. -/
instance isLeftAdjoint_upperStarFromPoint : (upperStarFromPoint X).IsLeftAdjoint :=
  (pointUpperStarLowerStarAdjunction.comp
    (ProEtale.upperStarLowerStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} (toSpecZ X))).isLeftAdjoint

/-- The cocone over the diagram `E ↦ E_X` with vertex `ℚ̄_ℓ,X`, given by the inclusions
`E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X`: the image under `p_X^*` of `Profinite.padicAlgClRingObjCocone`. -/
noncomputable abbrev padicAlgClRingObjCocone :
    Cocone (Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjFunctor ℓ ⋙ upperStarFromPoint X) :=
  (upperStarFromPoint X).mapCocone (Profinite.padicAlgClRingObjCocone ℓ)

/-- **`ℚ̄_ℓ,X = colim_E E_X`**: the inclusions `E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X` exhibit `ℚ̄_ℓ,X` as the filtered
colimit of the `E_X` in abelian pro-étale sheaves on `X`. This is the image of
`ℚ̲̄_ℓ = colim_E E̲` (`Profinite.isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone`) under the left adjoint
`p_X^*`. -/
noncomputable def isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone : IsColimit (padicAlgClRingObjCocone X ℓ) :=
  isColimitOfPreserves (upperStarFromPoint X) (Profinite.isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone ℓ)

end RingObj

namespace CondensedRing

variable (ℓ : ℕ) [Fact ℓ.Prime]

/-! ### Flatness of the coefficient inclusions -/

section Flat

variable (X : Scheme.{u})

/-- **(A0.2)** For finite subextensions `E ≤ F`, the extension of scalars along `E_X ⟶ F_X` is
exact: `F_X` is finite free, in particular flat, over `E_X`. See the module docstring. -/
instance preservesFiniteLimits_extendScalars_padicSubfieldRingObjMap
    {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    PreservesFiniteLimits
      (Mod.extendScalars (padicSubfieldRingObjMap X ℓ h)) :=
  sorry

/-- **(A0.2)** For a finite subextension `E`, the extension of scalars along `E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X` is
exact: `ℚ̄_ℓ,X` is flat over `E_X`, being a filtered colimit of finite free `E_X`-modules. See
the module docstring. -/
instance preservesFiniteLimits_extendScalars_padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl
    (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) :
    PreservesFiniteLimits
      (Mod.extendScalars (padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E)) :=
  sorry

end Flat

/-! ### (Push) and (Shriek) -/

section Push

variable {X Y : Scheme.{u}} (f : X ⟶ Y)

/-- **(Push)** is an isomorphism for a finite extension `F/E`, on all unbounded objects: `F_X`
is finite free over `E_X`, so both sides are finite direct sums of copies of `Rf_*^E`. See the
module docstring. -/
instance isIso_derivedPushHom_padicSubfieldRingObjMap {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ}
    (h : E ≤ F) : IsIso (derivedPushHom (Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjMap ℓ h) f) :=
  sorry

/-- **(Push)** is an isomorphism for `E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ` and `f` *proper*, on all unbounded objects: `Rf_*`
then admits a right adjoint `Rf^!`, hence commutes with the coproducts through which the derived
extension of scalars along the flat `E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X` is computed. See the module docstring. -/
instance isIso_derivedPushHom_padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl_of_isProper [IsProper f]
    (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) :
    IsIso (derivedPushHom (Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl ℓ E) f) :=
  sorry

variable [HasCompactification f]

/-- **(Shriek)** is an isomorphism for a finite extension `F/E`, on all unbounded objects. -/
example {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    IsIso (derivedLowerShriekHom (Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjMap ℓ h) f) :=
  inferInstance

/-- **(Shriek)** is an isomorphism for `E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ`, on all unbounded objects: the proper part of
the compactification contributes (Push) for a proper morphism, which is an isomorphism. -/
example (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) :
    IsIso (derivedLowerShriekHom (Profinite.padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl ℓ E) f) :=
  inferInstance

end Push

end CondensedRing

end AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale
```

</details>

<details>
<summary>WeilConjectures/SixFunctors/ProEtale/ModuleObject/Colimit/TwoColimit.lean</summary>

```lean
/-
Copyright (c) 2026 Nailin Guan. All rights reserved.
Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
Authors: Nailin Guan
-/
module

public import WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.FilteredTwoColimit
public import WeilConjectures.ConstructibleSheaf.ProEtaleModule.DerivedCategory
public import WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.Colimit.Compatibility

/-!

# The bounded constructible `ℚ̄_ℓ`-complexes as the 2-colimit over the finite subextensions

Let `ℓ` be a prime and `X` a scheme. For a finite subextension `E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ` of `ℚ_ℓ`
(`PadicAlgCl.FiniteIntermediateField ℓ`), let `C_E(X) = D^b_cons(X, E)` be the bounded
constructible derived category of modules over `E_X = p_X^* E̲`
(`ProEtale.BoundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)`, the full subcategory of the
unbounded `D(Mod_{E_X})` of `WeilConjectures.ConstructibleSheaf.ProEtaleModule.DerivedCategory`),
and let `C_Ω(X) = D^b_cons(X, ℚ̄_ℓ)` be the one of modules over `ℚ̄_ℓ,X = p_X^* ℚ̲̄_ℓ`. The
derived extensions of scalars
```
T^X_{E,F} = F_X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} - ,   T^X_{E,Ω} = ℚ̄_ℓ,X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} -
```
of `WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.Colimit.Compatibility` are
*assumed* to preserve bounded constructibility
(`ProEtale.boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsSubfield_obj`,
`ProEtale.boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl_obj`), and hence restrict to
functors
```
ProEtale.constructibleExtendScalars h : C_E(X) ⥤ C_F(X)   (E ≤ F),
ProEtale.constructibleToPadicAlgCl E : C_E(X) ⥤ C_Ω(X),
```
compatible with each other through the transitivity of extension of scalars
(`ProEtale.constructibleToPadicAlgClCompIso`). Together these form a cocone of categories
`ProEtale.constructibleCocone X ℓ` on the directed system `E ↦ C_E(X)`, with vertex `C_Ω(X)`,
in the sense of `WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.FilteredTwoColimit`.

The main statement, **`ℚ̄_ℓ` is a 2-colimit** (`ProEtale.constructibleCocone_isFilteredTwoColimit`),
is that this cocone is a filtered 2-colimit: the comparison functor
`2-colim_E C_E(X) ⟶ C_Ω(X)`, `(E, K_E) ↦ T^X_{E,Ω} K_E`, is an equivalence of ordinary
categories. By `CategoryTheory.CategoryCocone.IsFilteredTwoColimit` this is the conjunction of

* **(Obj)** `ProEtale.constructibleCocone_essSurjObj`: every bounded constructible
  `ℚ̄_ℓ,X`-complex `K` is isomorphic to `T^X_{E,Ω} K_E` for some finite subextension `E` and some
  bounded constructible `E_X`-complex `K_E` (descent of constructible objects);
* **(Hom)** `ProEtale.constructibleCocone_homSurj` and `ProEtale.constructibleCocone_homInj`:
  every morphism `T^X_{E,Ω} K_E ⟶ T^X_{F,Ω} L_F` in `D(Mod_{ℚ̄_ℓ,X})` is defined over some finite
  subextension `H ⊇ E, F`, and two morphisms over `H` with the same image are equal over some
  finite subextension `H' ⊇ H`. Since the bounded constructible complexes are stable under
  shifts, this includes the statement for the shifted targets `L_F[n]`, which is what the
  compatibility with the triangulated structures requires.

These are stated for a topologically noetherian `X`, the setting in which the bounded
constructible complexes form a triangulated subcategory
(`ProEtale.isTriangulated_boundedConstructible`). They are *not* formal consequences of the
identification `ℚ̄_ℓ,X ≃ colim_E E_X` of `ProEtale.isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone`, and they
are false for the unbounded derived categories; see the module docstring of
`WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.FilteredTwoColimit` for the ordinary-category model of the
2-colimit that they describe.

## Sorried input

* `ProEtale.boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsSubfield_obj` and
  `ProEtale.boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl_obj`: extension of scalars
  along `E_X ⟶ F_X` and `E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X` preserves bounded constructible complexes. The extension
  of scalars is exact
  (`ProEtale.CondensedRing.preservesFiniteLimits_extendScalars_padicSubfieldRingObjMap`), so it
  is computed termwise and preserves boundedness, and it sends a locally finitely
  presented module on a stratum to a locally finitely presented one, the presentation being
  tensored up.
* **(Obj)** `ProEtale.constructibleCocone_essSurjObj`: constructible descent. A bounded
  constructible `ℚ̄_ℓ,X`-complex has finitely many constructible cohomology sheaves; each is
  locally, on the strata of a stratification, finitely presented, and a finite presentation
  involves finitely many sections of `ℚ̄_ℓ,X`, which are defined over a finite subextension since
  `ℚ̄_ℓ,X = colim_E E_X` (`ProEtale.isColimitPadicAlgClRingObjCocone`). A finite Postnikov
  induction on the length of the complex then descends the complex itself, the gluing data being
  morphisms which descend by (Hom).
* **(Hom)** `ProEtale.constructibleCocone_homSurj` and `ProEtale.constructibleCocone_homInj`:
  continuity of the derived hom. For a bounded constructible first argument `K_E` and a
  bounded below second argument, `Hom_{D(Mod_{ℚ̄_ℓ,X})}(T_{E,Ω} K_E, T_{E,Ω} L_E)` is the filtered
  colimit over `H ⊇ E` of the `Hom_{D(Mod_{H_X})}(T_{E,H} K_E, T_{E,H} L_E)`: `K_E` is a finite
  extension of shifts of locally finitely presented modules, for which the derived hom into a
  filtered colimit of uniformly bounded below complexes is the filtered colimit of the derived
  homs, and `ℚ̄_ℓ,X = colim_H H_X`.

This is the input needed to transport base change theorems from finite to `ℚ̄_ℓ`-coefficients:
by (Obj) every constructible `K` is `T^X_{E,Ω} K_E`, and the compatibility of the base change
maps with the extension of scalars (built from the (Pull) and (Push) comparisons of
`WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.ChangeOfCoefficients`) then shows that the
base change map at `K` is the extension of scalars of the one at `K_E`. This compatibility of
the base change maps of `WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.BaseChangeTheorems`
with the extension of scalars is not yet formalized.

-/

@[expose] public section

universe u

open CategoryTheory Limits MonoidalCategory MonObj Abelian

open scoped CategoryTheory.Obj CategoryTheory.Functor

namespace AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale

open Scheme PadicAlgCl

variable (X : Scheme.{u}) (ℓ : ℕ) [Fact ℓ.Prime]

/-! ### The derived extensions of scalars and their transitivity -/

section ExtendScalars

/-- The derived extension of scalars `T^X_{E,F} = F_X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} - : D(Mod_{E_X}) ⥤ D(Mod_{F_X})`
along the inclusion of finite subextensions `E ≤ F`. -/
noncomputable abbrev derivedExtendScalarsSubfield {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    DerivedCategory (Mod (Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)) ⥤
      DerivedCategory (Mod (Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ F)) :=
  L(Mod.extendScalars (padicSubfieldRingObjMap X ℓ h))

/-- The derived extension of scalars
`T^X_{E,Ω} = ℚ̄_ℓ,X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} - : D(Mod_{E_X}) ⥤ D(Mod_{ℚ̄_ℓ,X})` along the inclusion of a finite
subextension `E` into `ℚ̄_ℓ`. -/
noncomputable abbrev derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) :
    DerivedCategory (Mod (Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)) ⥤
      DerivedCategory (Mod (Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (padicAlgClRingObj X ℓ)) :=
  L(Mod.extendScalars (padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E))

/-- Transitivity of the derived extension of scalars: `T^X_{E,F} ⋙ T^X_{F,Ω} ≅ T^X_{E,Ω}` for
`E ≤ F`. The extensions of scalars being exact, the composite of their derived functors is the
derived functor of the composite, which is the extension of scalars along the composite
inclusion `E_X ⟶ F_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X = E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X`. -/
noncomputable def derivedExtendScalarsCompIso {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    derivedExtendScalarsSubfield X ℓ h ⋙ derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl X ℓ F ≅
      derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E :=
  haveI : PreservesFiniteLimits (Mod.extendScalars (padicSubfieldRingObjMap X ℓ h ≫
      padicSubfieldRingObjToPadicAlgCl X ℓ F)) :=
    preservesFiniteLimits_of_natIso
      (Mod.extendScalarsIsoOfEq (padicSubfieldRingObjMap_comp_toPadicAlgCl X ℓ h)).symm
  (Functor.leftDerivedFunctorCompIso _ _).symm ≪≫
    Functor.leftDerivedFunctorNatIso _
      ((Mod.extendScalarsComp _ _).symm ≪≫
        Mod.extendScalarsIsoOfEq (padicSubfieldRingObjMap_comp_toPadicAlgCl X ℓ h))

end ExtendScalars

/-! ### The bounded constructible categories and the functors between them -/

section Constructible

/-- The bounded constructible derived category `C_E(X) = D^b_cons(X, E)` of `E_X`-modules, for
a finite subextension `E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ` of `ℚ_ℓ`. -/
noncomputable abbrev PadicSubfieldConstructible (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) : Type _ :=
  BoundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)

/-- The bounded constructible derived category `C_Ω(X) = D^b_cons(X, ℚ̄_ℓ)` of
`ℚ̄_ℓ,X`-modules. -/
noncomputable abbrev PadicAlgClConstructible : Type _ :=
  BoundedConstructible (padicAlgClRingObj X ℓ)

/-- Extension of scalars along `E_X ⟶ F_X` preserves bounded constructible complexes. See the
module docstring. -/
lemma boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsSubfield_obj {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ}
    (h : E ≤ F)
    (K : DerivedCategory (Mod (Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)))
    (hK : boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E) K) :
    boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ F)
      ((derivedExtendScalarsSubfield X ℓ h).obj K) :=
  sorry

/-- Extension of scalars along `E_X ⟶ ℚ̄_ℓ,X` preserves bounded constructible complexes. See the
module docstring. -/
lemma boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl_obj (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ)
    (K : DerivedCategory (Mod (Sheaf (ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)))
    (hK : boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E) K) :
    boundedConstructible (padicAlgClRingObj X ℓ) ((derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E).obj K) :=
  sorry

/-- The extension of scalars `T^X_{E,F} : C_E(X) ⥤ C_F(X)` on bounded constructible complexes,
for finite subextensions `E ≤ F`. -/
noncomputable def constructibleExtendScalars {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    PadicSubfieldConstructible X ℓ E ⥤ PadicSubfieldConstructible X ℓ F :=
  (boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ F)).lift
    ((boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)).ι ⋙ derivedExtendScalarsSubfield X ℓ h)
    (fun K ↦ boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsSubfield_obj X ℓ h K.obj K.property)

/-- The extension of scalars `T^X_{E,Ω} : C_E(X) ⥤ C_Ω(X)` on bounded constructible complexes,
for a finite subextension `E`: the functors of the cocone whose vertex is the bounded
constructible `ℚ̄_ℓ`-complexes. -/
noncomputable def constructibleToPadicAlgCl (E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ) :
    PadicSubfieldConstructible X ℓ E ⥤ PadicAlgClConstructible X ℓ :=
  (boundedConstructible (padicAlgClRingObj X ℓ)).lift
    ((boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)).ι ⋙ derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E)
    (fun K ↦ boundedConstructible_derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl_obj X ℓ E K.obj K.property)

/-- The functors to the bounded constructible `ℚ̄_ℓ`-complexes are compatible with the extensions
of scalars between finite subextensions: `T^X_{E,F} ⋙ T^X_{F,Ω} ≅ T^X_{E,Ω}` on bounded
constructible complexes, by `derivedExtendScalarsCompIso` and the full faithfulness of the
inclusion of the bounded constructible complexes. -/
noncomputable def constructibleToPadicAlgClCompIso {E F : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ} (h : E ≤ F) :
    constructibleExtendScalars X ℓ h ⋙ constructibleToPadicAlgCl X ℓ F ≅
      constructibleToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E :=
  Functor.fullyFaithfulCancelRight (boundedConstructible (padicAlgClRingObj X ℓ)).ι
    ((Iso.refl ((boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)).ι ⋙
        (derivedExtendScalarsSubfield X ℓ h ⋙ derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl X ℓ F)) :
      (constructibleExtendScalars X ℓ h ⋙ constructibleToPadicAlgCl X ℓ F) ⋙
          (boundedConstructible (padicAlgClRingObj X ℓ)).ι ≅
        (boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)).ι ⋙
          (derivedExtendScalarsSubfield X ℓ h ⋙ derivedExtendScalarsToPadicAlgCl X ℓ F)) ≪≫
      Functor.isoWhiskerLeft (boundedConstructible (padicSubfieldRingObj X ℓ E)).ι
        (derivedExtendScalarsCompIso X ℓ h))

/-- The cocone of categories `E ↦ C_E(X)` with vertex `C_Ω(X)`: the directed system of the
bounded constructible `E`-complexes over the finite subextensions `E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ` of `ℚ_ℓ`, with the
extensions of scalars as transition functors, and the extensions of scalars to `ℚ̄_ℓ` as
functors to the vertex. -/
noncomputable def constructibleCocone :
    CategoryCocone (fun E : FiniteIntermediateField ℓ ↦ PadicSubfieldConstructible X ℓ E)
      (PadicAlgClConstructible X ℓ) where
  T h := constructibleExtendScalars X ℓ h
  Φ E := constructibleToPadicAlgCl X ℓ E
  iso h := constructibleToPadicAlgClCompIso X ℓ h

end Constructible

/-! ### `ℚ̄_ℓ` is a 2-colimit -/

section TwoColimit

variable [TopologicalSpace.NoetherianSpace X]

/-- **(Obj)**: every bounded constructible `ℚ̄_ℓ,X`-complex is isomorphic to the extension of
scalars `T^X_{E,Ω} K_E` of a bounded constructible `E_X`-complex `K_E` for some finite
subextension `E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ` of `ℚ_ℓ`. See the module docstring. -/
theorem constructibleCocone_essSurjObj : (constructibleCocone X ℓ).EssSurjObj :=
  sorry

/-- **(Hom), surjectivity**: every morphism `T^X_{E,Ω} K_E ⟶ T^X_{F,Ω} L_F` between extensions of
scalars of bounded constructible complexes is the extension of scalars of a morphism
`T^X_{E,H} K_E ⟶ T^X_{F,H} L_F` for some finite subextension `H ⊇ E, F`. See the module
docstring. -/
theorem constructibleCocone_homSurj : (constructibleCocone X ℓ).HomSurj :=
  sorry

/-- **(Hom), injectivity**: two morphisms of bounded constructible `H_X`-complexes with the same
extension of scalars to `ℚ̄_ℓ,X` have the same extension of scalars to `H'_X` for some finite
subextension `H' ⊇ H`. See the module docstring. -/
theorem constructibleCocone_homInj : (constructibleCocone X ℓ).HomInj :=
  sorry

/-- **`ℚ̄_ℓ` is a 2-colimit**: the bounded constructible derived category `D^b_cons(X, ℚ̄_ℓ)`
of `ℚ̄_ℓ,X`-modules, with the extensions of scalars from the bounded constructible derived
categories `D^b_cons(X, E)` of the finite subextensions `E ⊆ ℚ̄_ℓ` of `ℚ_ℓ`, is the filtered
2-colimit of the latter: the comparison functor `2-colim_E D^b_cons(X, E) ⟶ D^b_cons(X, ℚ̄_ℓ)`,
`(E, K_E) ↦ ℚ̄_ℓ,X ⊗ᴸ_{E_X} K_E`, is an equivalence of ordinary categories. -/
theorem constructibleCocone_isFilteredTwoColimit :
    (constructibleCocone X ℓ).IsFilteredTwoColimit where
  essSurjObj := constructibleCocone_essSurjObj X ℓ
  homSurj := constructibleCocone_homSurj X ℓ
  homInj := constructibleCocone_homInj X ℓ

end TwoColimit

end AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale
```

</details>

<summary>WeilConjectures/SixFunctors/ProEtale/ModuleObject/LowerShriek/Defs.lean</summary>

```lean
/-
Copyright (c) 2026 Nailin Guan. All rights reserved.
Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
Authors: Nailin Guan
-/
module

public import WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.ModuleObjects.ProjectionFormula
public import WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.FiniteCoefficient.LowerShriek.Defs
public import WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.ModuleObject.UpperLowerStar.Defs

/-!

# Lower shriek on module objects over `Λ`

Let `Λ` be a commutative condensed ring. This file defines the extension by zero along open
immersions and the lower shriek along morphisms admitting a compactification on the categories
`Mod_{Λ_X}` of module objects over `Λ_X = p_X^* Λ`, from the corresponding functors on
abelian sheaves and the change of scalars along the canonical coefficient morphisms.

* For an open immersion `j : U ⟶ X`, the extension by zero `j_!` on abelian sheaves is left
  adjoint to the strong monoidal `j^*` and satisfies the projection formula
  `j_!(j^* L ⊗ M) ≅ L ⊗ j_! M` (`ProEtale.CondensedRing.hasProjectionFormula_openImmersion`,
  the geometric input of this file). Hence it lifts to a *linear* extension by zero
  `Mod_{j^* Λ_X} ⥤ Mod_{Λ_X}`, sending `M` to `j_! M` with the `Λ_X`-action
  `Λ_X ⊗ j_! M ≅ j_!(j^* Λ_X ⊗ M) ⟶ j_! M`
  (`ProEtale.CondensedRing.linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek`, the lift
  `Adjunction.mapModLeft` of `WeilConjectures.CategoryTheory.ModuleObjects.ProjectionFormula`).
  The extension by zero on module objects over `Λ_U` is the restriction of scalars along
  `α_j : j^* Λ_X ⟶ Λ_U` followed by the linear extension by zero,
  ```
  j_! = (linear j_!) ∘ Res_{α_j}
  ```
  (`ProEtale.CondensedRing.openImmersionLowerShriek`, notation `(j)_!open⟦Λ⟧`). Its
  underlying sheaf is `j_! M`, and it is exact. Since `α_j` is an isomorphism, `j_!` is left
  adjoint to `j^* = Ext_{α_j} ∘ mapMod(j^*)` on module objects
  (`ProEtale.CondensedRing.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction`): the adjunction
  `linear j_! ⊣ mapMod(j^*)` composed with `Res_{α_j} ⊣ Coext_{α_j}`, and
  `Coext_{α_j} ≅ Ext_{α_j}` for an isomorphism `α_j`.
* For a compactification `X ⟶[i] Z ⟶[g] Y` of `f : X ⟶ Y`, the lower shriek is
  `f_! := i_! ⋙ g_*` (`ProEtale.CondensedRing.compactificationLowerShriek`), and for `f`
  with a chosen compactification `HasCompactification.compactification f` it is
  `ProEtale.CondensedRing.lowerShriek`, notation `(f)_!⟦Λ⟧`, *definitionally* the
  composition for the chosen compactification (`ProEtale.CondensedRing.lowerShriek_eq`).

This is the module-object counterpart of
`WeilConjectures.SixFunctors.ProEtale.FiniteCoefficient.LowerShriek.Defs`.

## Sorried input

* `ProEtale.CondensedRing.hasProjectionFormula_openImmersion`: the projection formula for
  the extension by zero along an open immersion on abelian pro-étale sheaves, i.e. the canonical
  map `j_!(j^* L ⊗ M) ⟶ L ⊗ j_! M` is an isomorphism. Both sides are the sheaf supported on `U`
  whose restriction to `U` is `j^* L ⊗ M`, the tensor product of sheaves being computed by
  sheafification of the objectwise tensor product, which commutes with restriction to `U`.

-/

@[expose] public section

universe u

open CategoryTheory Limits MonoidalCategory

open scoped CategoryTheory.Obj

namespace AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.CondensedRing

open Scheme

variable (Λ : Sheaf (coherentTopology Profinite.{u}) Ab.{u + 1}) [MonObj Λ] [IsCommMonObj Λ]

/-! ### Extension by zero along an open immersion -/

section OpenImmersion

variable {U X : Scheme.{u}} (j : U ⟶ X) [IsOpenImmersion j]

/-- The projection formula for the extension by zero `j_!` along an open immersion on abelian
pro-étale sheaves: the canonical map `j_!(j^* L ⊗ M) ⟶ L ⊗ j_! M` is an isomorphism. See the
module docstring. -/
instance hasProjectionFormula_openImmersion :
    (ProEtale.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} j).HasProjectionFormula :=
  sorry

/-- The *linear* extension by zero `Mod_{j^* Λ_X} ⥤ Mod_{Λ_X}` along an open immersion
`j : U ⟶ X`: the lift of the extension by zero `j_!` on abelian sheaves to module objects
through the projection formula, sending `M` to `j_! M` with the `Λ_X`-action
`Λ_X ⊗ j_! M ≅ j_!(j^* Λ_X ⊗ M) ⟶ j_! M`. -/
noncomputable def linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek :
    Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology U) Ab.{u + 1})
      ((j)^*[Ab.{u + 1}].obj (condensedRingObj Λ X)) ⥤
      Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (condensedRingObj Λ X) :=
  (ProEtale.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} j).mapModLeft
    (condensedRingObj Λ X)

omit [IsCommMonObj Λ] in
/-- The underlying sheaf of the linear extension by zero is the extension by zero. -/
lemma linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek_comp_forget :
    linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek Λ j ⋙ Mod.forget (condensedRingObj Λ X) =
      Mod.forget _ ⋙ (j)_!open[Ab.{u + 1}] := rfl

/-- The linear extension by zero is left adjoint to the transport `mapMod(j^*)` of module
objects along the strong monoidal `j^*`. -/
noncomputable def linearOpenImmersionLowerShriekAdjunction :
    linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek Λ j ⊣ (j)^*[Ab.{u + 1}].mapMod (R := condensedRingObj Λ X) :=
  (ProEtale.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} j).mapModLeftAdjunction

instance linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek_additive : (linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek Λ j).Additive :=
  inferInstanceAs ((ProEtale.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} j).mapModLeft
    (condensedRingObj Λ X)).Additive

/-- The extension by zero on abelian sheaves preserves finite colimits, being a left adjoint. -/
instance preservesFiniteColimits_openImmersionLowerShriek_ab :
    PreservesFiniteColimits (j)_!open[Ab.{u + 1}] :=
  have : PreservesColimitsOfSize.{0, 0} (j)_!open[Ab.{u + 1}] :=
    (ProEtale.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} j
      ).leftAdjoint_preservesColimits
  inferInstance

/-- The linear extension by zero is left exact, since the extension by zero on abelian sheaves
is. -/
instance preservesFiniteLimits_linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek :
    PreservesFiniteLimits (linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek Λ j) :=
  inferInstanceAs (PreservesFiniteLimits
    ((ProEtale.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} j).mapModLeft
      (condensedRingObj Λ X)))

/-- The linear extension by zero is right exact. -/
instance preservesFiniteColimits_linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek :
    PreservesFiniteColimits (linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek Λ j) :=
  inferInstanceAs (PreservesFiniteColimits
    ((ProEtale.openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Ab.{u + 1} j).mapModLeft
      (condensedRingObj Λ X)))

/-- The extension by zero `j_! : Mod_{Λ_U} ⥤ Mod_{Λ_X}` along an open immersion
`j : U ⟶ X` on module objects over `Λ`: the restriction of scalars along
`α_j : j^* Λ_X ⟶ Λ_U` followed by the linear extension by zero. Its underlying sheaf is
the extension by zero `j_! M` of the underlying sheaf, with the `Λ_X`-action induced by the
projection formula and `α_j`. -/
noncomputable def openImmersionLowerShriek :
    Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology U) Ab.{u + 1}) (condensedRingObj Λ U) ⥤
      Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (condensedRingObj Λ X) :=
  Mod.comap (condensedPullbackHom Λ j) ⋙ linearOpenImmersionLowerShriek Λ j

@[inherit_doc openImmersionLowerShriek]
scoped notation "(" j ")_!open⟦" Λ "⟧" => openImmersionLowerShriek Λ j

omit [IsCommMonObj Λ] in
/-- The underlying sheaf of the extension by zero on module objects is the extension by zero
of the underlying sheaf. -/
lemma openImmersionLowerShriek_comp_forget :
    (j)_!open⟦Λ⟧ ⋙ Mod.forget (condensedRingObj Λ X) =
      Mod.forget (condensedRingObj Λ U) ⋙ (j)_!open[Ab.{u + 1}] := rfl

instance openImmersionLowerShriek_additive : (j)_!open⟦Λ⟧.Additive := by
  unfold openImmersionLowerShriek
  infer_instance

/-- The extension by zero on module objects along an open immersion is left exact. -/
instance preservesFiniteLimits_openImmersionLowerShriek :
    PreservesFiniteLimits (j)_!open⟦Λ⟧ := by
  unfold openImmersionLowerShriek
  exact comp_preservesFiniteLimits _ _

/-- The extension by zero on module objects along an open immersion is right exact. -/
instance preservesFiniteColimits_openImmersionLowerShriek :
    PreservesFiniteColimits (j)_!open⟦Λ⟧ := by
  unfold openImmersionLowerShriek
  exact comp_preservesFiniteColimits _ _

/-- The extension by zero on module objects along an open immersion is exact. -/
instance preservesHomology_openImmersionLowerShriek : (j)_!open⟦Λ⟧.PreservesHomology := {}

/-- The adjunction `j_! ⊣ j^*` on module objects along an open immersion: the adjunction
`linear j_! ⊣ mapMod(j^*)` composed with the adjunction `Res_{α_j} ⊣ Coext_{α_j}`, and
`Coext_{α_j} ≅ Ext_{α_j}` since `α_j` is an isomorphism. -/
noncomputable def openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction : (j)_!open⟦Λ⟧ ⊣ (j)^*⟦Λ⟧ :=
  ((Mod.restrictCoextendAdjunction (condensedPullbackHom Λ j)).comp
    (linearOpenImmersionLowerShriekAdjunction Λ j)).ofNatIsoRight
    (Functor.isoWhiskerLeft ((j)^*[Ab.{u + 1}].mapMod (R := condensedRingObj Λ X))
      (Mod.extendScalarsIsoCoextendScalars (condensedPullbackHom Λ j)).symm)

instance : (j)_!open⟦Λ⟧.IsLeftAdjoint :=
  (openImmersionLowerShriekUpperStarAdjunction Λ j).isLeftAdjoint

end OpenImmersion

/-! ### Lower shriek along a morphism admitting a compactification -/

section Compactification

variable {X Y : Scheme.{u}}

/-- The lower shriek on module objects associated with a compactification `X ⟶[i] Z ⟶[g] Y`
of `f`: the extension by zero `i_!` followed by the pushforward `g_*`. -/
noncomputable def compactificationLowerShriek {f : X ⟶ Y} (c : Compactification f) :
    Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (condensedRingObj Λ X) ⥤
      Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology Y) Ab.{u + 1}) (condensedRingObj Λ Y) :=
  (c.i)_!open⟦Λ⟧ ⋙ (c.g)_*⟦Λ⟧

variable (f : X ⟶ Y) [HasCompactification f]

/-- The lower shriek `f_! : Mod_{Λ_X} ⥤ Mod_{Λ_Y}` along a morphism `f : X ⟶ Y` admitting
a compactification, on module objects over `Λ`: the composition `i_! ⋙ g_*` associated
with the chosen compactification `HasCompactification.compactification f`. -/
noncomputable def lowerShriek :
    Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology X) Ab.{u + 1}) (condensedRingObj Λ X) ⥤
      Mod (Sheaf (Scheme.ProEt.topology Y) Ab.{u + 1}) (condensedRingObj Λ Y) :=
  compactificationLowerShriek Λ (HasCompactification.compactification f)

@[inherit_doc lowerShriek]
scoped notation "(" f ")_!⟦" Λ "⟧" => lowerShriek Λ f

omit [IsCommMonObj Λ] in
/-- The lower shriek `f_!` on module objects is the extension by zero along the open immersion
of the chosen compactification followed by the pushforward along its proper structure
morphism. -/
lemma lowerShriek_eq :
    (f)_!⟦Λ⟧ = ((HasCompactification.compactification f).i)_!open⟦Λ⟧ ⋙
      ((HasCompactification.compactification f).g)_*⟦Λ⟧ :=
  rfl

omit [IsCommMonObj Λ] in
/-- The underlying sheaf of `f_! M` is `f_! M`. -/
lemma lowerShriek_comp_forget :
    (f)_!⟦Λ⟧ ⋙ Mod.forget (condensedRingObj Λ Y) =
      Mod.forget (condensedRingObj Λ X) ⋙ ProEtale.lowerShriek Ab.{u + 1} f := rfl

instance lowerShriek_additive : (f)_!⟦Λ⟧.Additive := by
  rw [lowerShriek_eq]
  infer_instance

/-- The lower shriek `f_!` on module objects is left exact, being the composition of the exact
extension by zero along the open immersion of the chosen compactification with the left exact
pushforward along its proper structure morphism. -/
instance preservesFiniteLimits_lowerShriek : PreservesFiniteLimits (f)_!⟦Λ⟧ := by
  rw [lowerShriek_eq]
  exact comp_preservesFiniteLimits _ _

end Compactification

end AlgebraicGeometry.ProEtale.CondensedRing
```
